# Shadows of War - Absolute Complete Leaders Roster

This file documents the complete roster of **1269** leaders for the game. Each leader has detailed Hero and Empire descriptions designed for high-end AAA character concept art.

---

## Original Leaders (1–25)

### 1. Babur | Mughal Empire | 1483–1530 CE
- **Hero:** Compact muscular build in Persian-style turban and fitted coat of deep crimson, one hand holding a stylized book of poetry, the other on his dagger, refined but fierce eyes, cultured warlord's smirk, stance light and ready.
- **Empire:** Red Fort walls and Mughal gardens with geometric water channels, Indian plains with rounded mango trees, distant Hindu Kush mountains in soft purple haze, peacocks and Persian-Indian architectural fusion, warm subcontinental light.

### 2. Ramses II | New Kingdom Egypt | 1303–1213 BCE
- **Hero:** Towering broad-shouldered physique in pleated white and gold linen kilt, blue-and-gold striped nemes crown, holding a bronze khopesh sword. Intense god-king arrogance, sharp jawline, commanding and immortal stance.
- **Empire:** Colossal statues of Abu Simbel carved into chunky sandstone cliffs, the Nile river with stylized felucca boats, lush papyrus reeds, sweeping desert dunes under a blazing midday sun, gold and turquoise palette.

### 3. Hannibal Barca | Carthaginian Empire | 247–183 BCE
- **Hero:** Rugged, battle-hardened build, bronze Hellenistic breastplate over dark purple tunic, eyepatch over one eye, resting hand on a heavy sword hilt. Tactical genius expression, brooding and unyielding, predator's patience.
- **Empire:** Snow-capped jagged Alps passes, massive war elephants with chunky stylized armor, Carthaginian shield banners, distant Mediterranean coast, slate gray and deep purple atmosphere.

### 4. Cyrus the Great | Achaemenid Persian Empire | 600–530 BCE
- **Hero:** Tall imposing frame, fluted Persian tiara, elaborate multi-layered robes in deep lapis and gold, holding a stylized golden scepter. Benevolent but absolute authority, wise piercing eyes, calm and magnetic presence.
- **Empire:** Persepolis ruins with massive bull-headed capitals, grand staircases with chunky stone reliefs, Persian gardens, distant Zagros mountains, twilight indigo and gold palette.

### 5. Vercingetorix | Gallic Tribes | 82–46 BCE
- **Hero:** Massive chest, bare torso painted with faint woad, thick winged helmet, heavy torc, gripping a long iron broadsword. Defiant, ferocious rebel yell expression, eyes burning with untamed liberty.
- **Empire:** Gergovia hilltop fortress with wooden palisades, dark chunky European pine forests, misty Gaulish valleys, gathering storm clouds, earthy browns and stormy grey palette.

### 6. Trajan | Roman Empire | 53–117 CE
- **Hero:** Square-jawed and barrel-chested, ornate imperial lorica segmentata, crimson general's cloak, holding a rolled parchment. Pragmatic, stoic builder, eyes radiating civic duty and military supremacy.
- **Empire:** Trajan's Column rising above chunky marble forums, busy Roman aqueducts, Dacian spoils, bustling marble streets under a bright, crisp Mediterranean sky, imperial red and white palette.

### 7. Harun al-Rashid | Abbasid Caliphate | 763–809 CE
- **Hero:** Broad regal build, lavish emerald silk robes with golden geometric trim, pristine white turban with a central ruby, holding an astrolabe. Scholarly majesty, culturally enlightened, mysterious and powerful gaze.
- **Empire:** Round city of Baghdad with massive mud-brick walls, chunky golden domes, Tigris river with trade dhows, flying carpets of scholars, starlit Arabian night, emerald and midnight blue palette.

### 8. Clovis I | Frankish Kingdom | 466–511 CE
- **Hero:** Thick muscular frame, wolf-fur mantle over chainmail, long braided blonde hair, leaning on a heavy bearded axe (francisca). Brutal, pragmatic king-maker, cunning and ambitious smirk.
- **Empire:** Dark Age European woodlands, early chunky stone monasteries, Frankish wooden halls, distant Rhine river, misty morning with sharp shafts of sunlight, iron and forest green palette.

### 9. Chandragupta Maurya | Mauryan Empire | 340–297 BCE
- **Hero:** Lithe and athletic, golden scale armor over saffron silk, jeweled turban, holding a curved Indian blade. Ascetic discipline mixed with conqueror's ambition, focused and piercing eyes.
- **Empire:** Pataliputra wooden palisades, massive stylized Indian elephants, Ganges river plains, intricate Vedic temples, lush tropical canopy, vibrant saffron and emerald palette.

### 10. Sejong the Great | Joseon Dynasty (Korea) | 1397–1450 CE
- **Hero:** Tall and composed, red dragon-embroidered royal robes (gonryongpo), winged black silk hat. Benevolent scholar-king, warm but incredibly sharp intellectual eyes, holding a stylized Hangul manuscript.
- **Empire:** Gyeongbokgung palace with chunky sweeping tiled roofs, autumn maple trees, distant stylized Korean mountain peaks, clear blue sky, crimson and autumn gold palette.

### 11. Jayavarman VII | Khmer Empire | 1122–1218 CE
- **Hero:** Broad and muscular, bare chest with ornate gold armbands and heavy collars, serene Buddhist smile (the "Angkor smile"), eyes half-closed in meditation but radiating absolute power.
- **Empire:** Angkor Thom massive stone faces overgrown by chunky banyan tree roots, lotus-filled moats, towering sandstone prangs, steamy jungle atmosphere, moss green and grey stone palette.

### 12. Gustavus Adolphus | Swedish Empire | 1594–1632 CE
- **Hero:** Barrel-chested, thick blonde goatee, wearing a buff leather coat over a steel cuirass, holding a flintlock pistol. The "Lion of the North", fierce Protestant zeal, commanding and fearless battlefield eyes.
- **Empire:** Snowy Baltic coast, chunky wooden Swedish warships, regimented lines of musketeers, winter pine forests, icy blue and steel grey palette.

### 13. Akbar the Great | Mughal Empire | 1542–1605 CE
- **Hero:** Powerful athletic build, jeweled turban with a heron plume, emerald and gold jama coat, holding a hunting falcon on one armored wrist. Tolerant, curious, yet fiercely dominant gaze.
- **Empire:** Fatehpur Sikri red sandstone palaces, royal hunting grounds, majestic stylized tigers, vibrant sunset over the plains, deep crimson and sandstone palette.

### 14. Moctezuma I | Aztec Empire | 1398–1469 CE
- **Hero:** Imposing and fierce, massive quetzal feather headdress, jaguar skin armor, holding a chunky obsidian macuahuitl club. Divine emperor authority, intimidating predator eyes, rigid and proud stance.
- **Empire:** Tenochtitlan step pyramids rising from Lake Texcoco, stylized chinampa floating gardens, causeways, volcanic peaks in the distance, vibrant jade, gold, and blood-red palette.

### 15. Richard the Lionheart | Angevin Empire (England) | 1157–1199 CE
- **Hero:** Towering, massive chest, heavy chainmail under a white surcoat with three golden lions, leaning on a massive broadsword. Crusader zeal, reckless bravery, scarred and determined face.
- **Empire:** Chunky stone crusader castles (Krak des Chevaliers), burning siege engines, arid Levantine desert, sweeping English banners, dusty yellow and striking red palette.

### 16. Atahualpa | Inca Empire | 1502–1533 CE
- **Hero:** Thick-set, powerful limbs, massive gold earplugs, vicuna wool uncu (tunic) with checkerboard patterns, holding a golden sun halberd. Arrogant, divine confidence, sharp untamed mountain king gaze.
- **Empire:** Cajamarca hot springs, massive chunky polygonal stone walls, soaring condors, dramatic Andean mountain passes, highland green and solid gold palette.

### 17. Pacal the Great | Maya Civilization | 603–683 CE
- **Hero:** Athletic, jade mosaic death mask worn pushed back on the head, intricate jade pectorals and loincloth, holding a stylized double-headed serpent bar. Mystical, deeply spiritual, commanding the cosmos.
- **Empire:** Palenque Temple of the Inscriptions, towering chunky step pyramids emerging from dense jungle canopy, stylized ceiba trees, steamy tropical mist, jade and limestone palette.

### 18. Otto I | Holy Roman Empire | 912–973 CE
- **Hero:** Broad-shouldered, heavy iron crown, thick fur-lined cloak over plate armor, holding the Holy Lance. Stern, unifying Germanic warlord, eyes full of iron will and religious mandate.
- **Empire:** Chunky Romanesque cathedrals, dark Bavarian forests, snowy Alpine foothills, medieval knights on barded horses, cold steel and snow-white palette.

### 19. Suleiman the Magnificent | Ottoman Empire | 1494–1566 CE
- **Hero:** Regal imposing stature, massive white onion turban, opulent Ottoman caftan in silk, jeweled dagger at waist. Supreme lawgiver, refined imperial dominance, penetrating wise eyes.
- **Empire:** Blue Mosque silhouettes, chunky minarets, Bosphorus strait with Ottoman galleys, vibrant Turkish carpets draped on balconies, turquoise and gold palette.

### 20. Hammurabi | Babylonian Empire | 1810–1750 BCE
- **Hero:** Sturdy and grounded, long stylized curled beard, layered fringed woolen robes, holding a stone tablet and stylus. The absolute lawgiver, stern, uncompromising, eyes evaluating justice.
- **Empire:** Massive ziggurats of Babylon, chunky cuneiform steles, hanging gardens, the Euphrates river, dusty Mesopotamian sun, baked clay and lapis lazuli palette.

### 21. Kamehameha I | Kingdom of Hawaii | 1758–1819 CE
- **Hero:** Massive, physically dominating Polynesian warrior, striking red and yellow feather cloak ('ahu 'ula) and helmet (mahiole), holding a shark-tooth club. Fierce unifying conqueror, proud and unbreakable spirit.
- **Empire:** Volcanic Hawaiian peaks, chunky stylized lava flows, crashing Pacific waves, outrigger canoes, lush tropical cliffs, fiery red and deep ocean blue palette.

### 22. Ashurbanipal | Neo-Assyrian Empire | 668–627 BCE
- **Hero:** Muscular and heavily armored in bronze scales, intricate braided square beard, drawing a massive recurve bow. Cruel, highly educated apex predator, cold calculating eyes.
- **Empire:** Nineveh winged bull statues (lamassu) in chunky stone, royal lion hunt arenas, mud-brick palace walls, Tigris river, bronze and desert dust palette.

### 23. Tokugawa Ieyasu | Tokugawa Shogunate (Japan) | 1543–1616 CE
- **Hero:** Stocky, immovable stance, heavy black and gold samurai armor, holding a closed war fan. The ultimate patient strategist, calculating, patient eyes waiting for the right moment.
- **Empire:** Edo castle with chunky sweeping roofs, stylized pine trees, disciplined ranks of ashigaru (musketeers), Mount Fuji in the background, stark black, gold, and winter white palette.

### 24. Louis XIV | Kingdom of France | 1638–1715 CE
- **Hero:** Elegant but broad-shouldered, towering curled wig, lavish silk coat with fleur-de-lis embroidery, holding a golden sun scepter. The Sun King, absolute divine right, arrogant and radiant smirk.
- **Empire:** Palace of Versailles, chunky geometric hedge gardens, ornate golden fountains, courtiers, bright sunny sky, royal blue, gold, and pristine white palette.

### 25. Sitting Bull | Lakota Sioux | 1831–1890 CE
- **Hero:** Broad chest, deeply weathered and wise face, full eagle-feather headdress, holding a traditional pipe and a Winchester rifle. Resolute spiritual leader, eyes full of tragic defiance and deep wisdom.
- **Empire:** Black Hills of South Dakota, rolling plains of tall grass, massive buffalo herds, stylized painted tipis, dramatic stormy sky, earth tones and vibrant sky blue palette.
